  type?: "IPS" | "Backbone Switch" | "System" | "Firewall" | "Wireless Switch" | "Data Link";
};

function Device({ className, type = "Backbone Switch" }: DeviceProps) {
  const isBackboneSwitch = type === "Backbone Switch";
  const isBackboneSwitchOrIps = ["Backbone Switch", "IPS"].includes(type);
  const isDataLink = type === "Data Link";
  const isFirewall = type === "Firewall";
  const isIps = type === "IPS";
  const isSystem = type === "System";
  const isSystemOrIps = ["System", "IPS"].includes(type);
  const isWirelessSwitch = type === "Wireless Switch";
  const isWirelessSwitchOrDataLink = ["Wireless Switch", "Data Link"].includes(type);
  return (
    <div className={className || `relative ${isDataLink ? "h-[97.489px] w-[110px]" : isIps ? "h-[110.691px] w-[110px]" : isWirelessSwitch ? "h-[73.567px] w-[120px]" : isFirewall ?
